# Developer-7 — Interview Question Completion Checklist

> **Personal progress:** **0 / 2,719 completed**. Every box starts unchecked, regardless of the original Developer-7 roadmap's completion status.

Track **each unique question exactly once** in original source order. Exact duplicate appearances are merged; an item's **Q-number is permanent** and corresponds to the [tracker question catalog](data.js). Questions repeated in other sets list their other set numbers on the same row.

**How to update:** Tell ChatGPT e.g. **“I finished Q0007 and Q0023”**, or paste a question / set number and identify which ones you finished. I can then mark the confirmed items `- [x]` in this GitHub file and update the completed count. Marking this file does **not** automatically change the separate browser-only [TRACE dashboard](index.html).

**Catalog audit:** 3,989 question appearances → **2,719 unique questions**; 1,270 repeated appearances consolidated; 390 numbered sets + 9 original appendix questions. Similar but differently phrased questions were left as separate items.

---
